[31m-<!-- HERO -->[m
[31m-<section class="hero">[m
[31m-  <div class="inner">[m
[31m-    <div class="hero-badge">Germany Visa 2026</div>[m
[31m-    <h1>Berlin Visa Requirements —<br><em>What Actually Determines Approval</em></h1>[m
[31m-    <p>[m
[31m-      The official requirements list is easy to find. What isn't easy to find[m
[31m-      is why two professionals with identical documents get different outcomes —[m
[31m-      and what separates a strong application from one that stalls for months.[m
[31m-      This is what we've learned from over 500 cases since 2012.[m
[31m-    </p>[m
[31m-  </div>[m
[31m-</section>[m
[31m-[m
[31m-<!-- DISTINCTION: reframes the reader's question -->[m
[31m-<section class="section">[m
[31m-  <div class="inner">[m
[31m-    <p class="section-label">First, the right question</p>[m
[31m-    <h2 style="font-size:clamp(20px,3.2vw,26px);font-weight:700;margin-bottom:20px">[m
[31m-      "Visa requirements" is the wrong starting point[m
[31m-    </h2>[m
[31m-    <div class="distinction">[m
[31m-      <p>[m
[31m-        Most people searching for "Berlin visa requirements" are actually asking[m
[31m-        a different question: <strong>which visa fits my situation, and what are[m
[31m-        my realistic chances of approval?</strong> The requirements list for each[m
[31m-        visa type is straightforward. What's less obvious is which category[m
[31m-        applies to your specific profile — and that single classification decision[m
[31m-        determines everything that follows.[m
[31m-      </p>[m
[31m-    </div>[m
[31m-    <p style="font-size:16px;color:#374151;line-height:1.8;font-weight:300">[m
[31m-      For skilled professionals moving to Berlin for work, there are two paths[m
[31m-      that matter. Every other visa type (tourist, student, au pair) is either[m
[31m-      irrelevant to your situation or not a path to long-term residence.[m
[31m-    </p>[m
[31m-  </div>[m
[31m-</section>[m
[31m-[m
[31m-<!-- TWO PATHS -->[m
[31m-<section class="section section--alt">[m
[31m-  <div class="inner">[m
[31m-    <p class="section-label">The Two Paths That Matter</p>[m
[31m-    <h2 style="font-size:clamp(20px,3.2vw,26px);font-weight:700;margin-bottom:8px">[m
[31m-      Chancenkarte vs. Skilled Worker Visa[m
[31m-    </h2>[m
[31m-    <p style="font-size:16px;color:#6B7280;margin-bottom:4px;font-weight:300">[m
[31m-      Both lead to Berlin. The difference is where you start.[m
[31m-    </p>[m
[31m-[m
[31m-    <div class="visa-grid">[m
[31m-      <div class="visa-card primary">[m
[31m-        <h4>Chancenkarte — Opportunity Card</h4>[m
[31m-        <span class="tag tag--primary">No job offer required</span>[m
[31m-        <p>[m
[31m-          A points-based visa that lets you enter Germany and job-search[m
[31m-          from inside the country. You need at least 6 points across[m
[31m-          four criteria: qualification level, work experience, language[m
[31m-          skills, and age.[m
[31m-        </p>[m
[31m-        <div class="insight">[m
[31m-          <strong>What most people miss:</strong> Your work experience[m
[31m-          earned anywhere in the world counts — including Gulf countries.[m
[31m-          A professional with 5+ years in UAE with a recognized degree[m
[31m-          often qualifies without any German language requirement.[m
[31m-        </div>[m
[31m-      </div>[m
[31m-      <div class="visa-card secondary">[m
[31m-        <h4>Skilled Worker Visa (Fachkräftevisa)</h4>[m
[31m-        <span class="tag tag--secondary">Job offer required</span>[m
[31m-        <p>[m
[31m-          If you already have a contract from a German employer, this[m
[31m-          is the faster path. Processing time: 4–8 weeks in most cases.[m
[31m-          Recognition of your qualification (Anerkennung) may or may[m
[31m-          not be required depending on your profession.[m
[31m-        </p>[m
[31m-        <div class="insight">[m
[31m-          <strong>What most people miss:</strong> For IT professionals[m
[31m-          and many engineering roles, formal recognition is not required —[m
[31m-          demonstrated experience is sufficient. For healthcare (doctors,[m
[31m-          nurses), recognition is mandatory before the visa is issued.[m
[31m-        </div>[m
[31m-      </div>[m
[31m-    </div>[m
[31m-  </div>[m
[31m-</section>[m
[31m-[m
[31m-<!-- REQUIREMENTS TABLE -->[m

[31m-    </h2>[m
[31m-    <p style="font-size:15px;color:#6B7280;margin-bottom:4px;font-weight:300">[m
[31m-      These are the standard requirements. What varies — and where most[m
[31m-      applications run into problems — is document preparation and timing.[m
[31m-    </p>[m

[32m+[m[32m[31m-    <p class="lead">Das deutsche System unterscheidet strikt. Wir zeigen Ihnen, in welche Kategorie Sie fallen.</p>[m[m

[32m+[m[32m[31m-    <h2>Anerkennung — FAQ</h2>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-    <p class="lead">Antworten auf die wichtigsten Fragen zum Anerkennungsprozess.</p>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-    [m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-    <ul class="faq-list">[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-      <li class="faq-item">[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        <div class="faq-question">[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-          <span>Wie lange dauert das gesamte Verfahren?</span>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-          <span class="faq-toggle">▼</span>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        </div>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        <div class="faq-answer">[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-          <p>Ein reguläres Verfahren dauert bei den Behörden meist 3–4 Monate, sobald alle Unterlagen vollständig vorliegen. Die Vorbereitung der Dokumente nimmt oft weitere 1–2 Monate in Anspruch.</p>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        </div>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-      </li>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-      <li class="faq-item">[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        <div class="faq-question">[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-          <span>Was kostet die Anerkennung?</span>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-          <span class="faq-toggle">▼</span>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        </div>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        <div class="faq-answer">[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-          <p>Die Gebühren der Behörden liegen meist zwischen 200 € und 600 €. Hinzu kommen Kosten für Übersetzungen und Beglaubigungen. Wir geben Ihnen eine detaillierte Kostenschätzung.</p>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        </div>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-      </li>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-      <li class="faq-item">[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        <div class="faq-question">[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-          <span>Kann ich ohne Anerkennung ein Visum bekommen?</span>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-          <span class="faq-toggle">▼</span>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        </div>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        <div class="faq-answer">[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-          <p>Für reglementierte Berufe: Nein. Für nicht-reglementierte Berufe ist es unter bestimmten Bedingungen (z.B. IT-Spezialisten mit Erfahrung) möglich, aber schwieriger.</p>[m[m

[32m+[m[32m[31m-    <p class="section-label">Häufig gestellte Fragen</p>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-    <h2>Voraussetzungen — FAQ</h2>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-    <p class="lead">Finden Sie Antworten auf die wichtigsten Fragen zu den Anforderungen.</p>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-    [m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-    <ul class="faq-list">[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-      <li class="faq-item">[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        <div class="faq-question">[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-          <span>Reicht ein Bachelor-Abschluss aus?</span>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-          <span class="faq-toggle">▼</span>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        </div>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        <div class="faq-answer">[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-          <p>Ja, in den meisten Fällen ist ein Bachelor-Abschluss ausreichend, sofern er von einer in Deutschland anerkannten Universität stammt. Wir prüfen dies über das Anabin-System.</p>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        </div>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-      </li>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-      <li class="faq-item">[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        <div class="faq-question">[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-          <span>Muss ich bereits Deutsch sprechen?</span>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-          <span class="faq-toggle">▼</span>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        </div>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        <div class="faq-answer">[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-          <p>Für die Bewertung nicht unbedingt, aber für den Erfolg in Deutschland ja. Mindestens B1 ist für fast alle Berufe die Untergrenze.</p>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        </div>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-      </li>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-      <li class="faq-item">[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        <div class="faq-question">[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-          <span>Was ist eine beglaubigte Übersetzung?</span>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-          <span class="faq-toggle">▼</span>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        </div>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        <div class="faq-answer">[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-          <p>Eine Übersetzung durch einen in Deutschland vereidigten Übersetzer. Nur diese werden von deutschen Behörden offiziell anerkannt.</p>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        </div>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-      </li>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-      <li class="faq-item">[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        <div class="faq-question">[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-          <span>Gilt meine Berufserfahrung aus dem Ausland?</span>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-          <span class="faq-toggle">▼</span>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        </div>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        <div class="faq-answer">[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-          <p>Ja, sofern sie nachweisbar ist. Arbeitszeugnisse und Referenzen sind hierfür entscheidend.</p>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        </div>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-      </li>[m[m

[32m+[m[32m[31m-  <div class="inner">[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-    <p class="section-label">First, the right question</p>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-    <h2 style="font-size:clamp(20px,3.2vw,26px);font-weight:700;margin-bottom:20px">[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-      "Visa requirements" is the wrong starting point[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-    </h2>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-    <div class="distinction">[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-      <p>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        Most people searching for "Berlin visa requirements" are actually asking[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        a different question: <strong>which visa fits my situation, and what are[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        my realistic chances of approval?</strong> The requirements list for each[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        visa type is straightforward. What's less obvious is which category[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        applies to your specific profile — and that single classification decision[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        determines everything that follows.[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-      </p>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-    </div>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-    <p style="font-size:16px;color:#374151;line-height:1.8;font-weight:300">[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-      For skilled professionals moving to Berlin for work, there are two paths[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-      that matter. Every other visa type (tourist, student, au pair) is either[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-      irrelevant to your situation or not a path to long-term residence.[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-    </p>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-  </div>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-</section>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-<!-- TWO PATHS -->[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-<section class="section section--alt">[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-  <div class="inner">[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-    <p class="section-label">The Two Paths That Matter</p>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-    <h2 style="font-size:clamp(20px,3.2vw,26px);font-weight:700;margin-bottom:8px">[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-      Chancenkarte vs. Skilled Worker Visa[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-    </h2>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-    <p style="font-size:16px;color:#6B7280;margin-bottom:4px;font-weight:300">[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-      Both lead to Berlin. The difference is where you start.[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-    </p>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-    <div class="visa-grid">[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-      <div class="visa-card primary">[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        <h4>Chancenkarte — Opportunity Card</h4>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        <span class="tag tag--primary">No job offer required</span>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        <p>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-          A points-based visa that lets you enter Germany and job-search[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-          from inside the country. You need at least 6 points across[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-          four criteria: qualification level, work experience, language[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-          skills, and age.[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        </p>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        <div class="insight">[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-          <strong>What most people miss:</strong> Your work experience[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-          earned anywhere in the world counts — including Gulf countries.[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-          A professional with 5+ years in UAE with a recognized degree[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-          often qualifies without any German language requirement.[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        </div>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-      </div>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-      <div class="visa-card secondary">[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        <h4>Skilled Worker Visa (Fachkräftevisa)</h4>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        <span class="tag tag--secondary">Job offer required</span>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        <p>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-          If you already have a contract from a German employer, this[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-          is the faster path. Processing time: 4–8 weeks in most cases.[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-          Recognition of your qualification (Anerkennung) may or may[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-          not be required depending on your profession.[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        </p>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        <div class="insight">[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-          <strong>What most people miss:</strong> For IT professionals[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-          and many engineering roles, formal recognition is not required —[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-          demonstrated experience is sufficient. For healthcare (doctors,[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-          nurses), recognition is mandatory before the visa is issued.[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-        </div>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-      </div>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-    </div>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-  </div>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-</section>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-<!-- REQUIREMENTS TABLE -->[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-<section class="section">[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-  <div class="inner">[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-    <p class="section-label">Document Requirements</p>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-    <h2 style="font-size:clamp(20px,3.2vw,26px);font-weight:700;margin-bottom:8px">[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-      What you actually need — by visa type[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-    </h2>[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-    <p style="font-size:15px;color:#6B7280;margin-bottom:4px;font-weight:300">[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-      These are the standard requirements. What varies — and where most[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-      applications run into problems — is document preparation and timing.[m[m
[32m+[m[32m[31m-    </p>[m[m
